    // Pure versions of the three training models — no DOM reads, no HTML.
    function simDailyRegen(c, useRefill) {
        const natTrains = Math.floor(c.natE * 24 / c.ePerTrain);
        const nat = simulateBlock(c.dots, c.statNow, c.propHappy, c.bonus, c.ePerTrain, c.consts, natTrains, c.propHappy);
        const ref = useRefill
            ? simulateBlock(c.dots, nat.finalStat, c.propHappy, c.bonus, c.ePerTrain, c.consts, Math.floor(c.cap/c.ePerTrain), c.propHappy)
            : { totalGain: 0 };
        return { gain: nat.totalGain + ref.totalGain, cycleH: 24 };
    }

    // Generalised drug-grind day. Xanax and LSD share ONE drug cooldown, so a day
    // is a race for CD slots: Xanax buys 250E per slot, LSD only 50E — but LSD's
    // slot is ~3h against Xanax's ~7h, it never ODs, and per energy it's roughly
    // half the price. Which wins depends on live prices, so both get a rung.
    //
    // Energy per cycle = regen across the cooldown + the drug's own energy. Regen
    // is uncapped by the bar because an attentive player trains mid-cycle to drain
    // it (that's what calculateXanaxGrind models as its "mid-CD nat train").
    function simDrugDay(c, doses, energyPer, cdHrs, useRefill) {
        const perCycleE = cdHrs * c.natE + energyPer;
        const cycleTr   = Math.floor(perCycleE / c.ePerTrain);
        const leftE     = Math.min(c.cap, Math.max(0, 24 - doses * cdHrs) * c.natE);
        const leftTr    = Math.floor(leftE / c.ePerTrain);

        let cur = c.statNow, gain = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < doses; i++) {
            const b = simulateBlock(c.dots, cur, c.propHappy, c.bonus, c.ePerTrain, c.consts, cycleTr, c.propHappy);
            gain += b.totalGain; cur = b.finalStat;
        }
        if (useRefill) {
            const r = simulateBlock(c.dots, cur, c.propHappy, c.bonus, c.ePerTrain, c.consts, Math.floor(c.cap/c.ePerTrain), c.propHappy);
            gain += r.totalGain; cur = r.finalStat;
        }
        gain += simulateBlock(c.dots, cur, c.propHappy, c.bonus, c.ePerTrain, c.consts, leftTr, c.propHappy).totalGain;
        return { gain, cycleH: 24 };
    }

    // ── Booster cooldown capacity ────────────────────────────────────────────
    // Torn checks whether your CURRENT booster cooldown is below the cap, not
    // where the item would land you. So the final item always overshoots, and you
    // fit exactly one more than cap/itemCD suggests. Confirmed two ways:
    //   24h cap ÷ 6h eDVD = 4, +1 = 5 eDVD  → the standard jump every guide lists
    //   48h cap (full Voracity)     = 8, +1 = 9 eDVD = 22,500 happy → the known max
    // Same rule gives 49 candy at 0.5h each — which is why the guides all say 49,
    // and why this script's own price panel was right to say "x49".
    // Voracity adds 1h per upgrade, 24 upgrades max, so the cap tops out at 48h.
    function boosterFit(capHrs, itemCD, usedCD = 0) {
        const room = capHrs - usedCD;
        if (room <= 0 || itemCD <= 0) return 0;
        // Epsilon must be ADDED, not subtracted: the interesting cases divide
        // exactly (24/6, 48/6, 24/0.5) and subtracting would round them down.
        return Math.floor(room / itemCD + 1e-9) + 1;
    }

    function simJump(c, cfg) {
        const xan   = cfg.xanax   || 0, edvd = cfg.edvd || 0, fhc = cfg.fhc || 0;
        const lsd   = cfg.lsd     || 0, cans = cfg.cans || 0, candies = cfg.candies || 0;
        const canE  = cfg.canType   ? Math.round(cfg.canType.e * (1 + c.canFacPerk)) : 0;
        const candyH= cfg.candyType ? Math.round(cfg.candyType.happy * c.candyMult)  : 0;

        let happy = c.propHappy + edvd * (c.anJob ? EDVD_HAPPY*2 : EDVD_HAPPY)
                  + fhc * FHC_HAPPY + candies * candyH;
        if (cfg.ecstasy) happy = Math.min(happy * 2, 99999);

        const xanE    = xan === 4 ? 1000 : Math.min(1000, c.cap + xan * 250);
        const stackE  = Math.min(1000, xanE + lsd * 50 + cans * canE);
        const stackTr = Math.floor(stackE / c.ePerTrain);
        const fhcTr   = Math.floor(fhc * c.cap / c.ePerTrain);

        const sb = simulateBlock(c.dots, c.statNow, happy, c.bonus, c.ePerTrain, c.consts, stackTr);
        const fb = fhc > 0
            ? simulateBlock(c.dots, c.statNow + sb.totalGain, sb.finalHappy + fhc * FHC_HAPPY, c.bonus, c.ePerTrain, c.consts, fhcTr)
            : { totalGain: 0 };
        const rb = cfg.refill
            ? simulateBlock(c.dots, c.statNow + sb.totalGain + fb.totalGain, c.propHappy, c.bonus, c.ePerTrain, c.consts, Math.floor(c.cap/c.ePerTrain))
            : { totalGain: 0 };

        // Drug and booster cooldowns are SEPARATE timers in Torn, so the cycle is
        // gated by whichever runs longer, not by their sum.
        //   drug   — 4 Xanax can't be taken at once; you accumulate them over ~4×7h
        //   booster— eDVD/FHC/candy/cans share one pool, capped at 24h + Voracity
        // Summing them (as the older Happy Jump tab does) makes Voracity *lengthen*
        // the cycle, i.e. buying the perk would look like a downgrade. It isn't.
        // Booster CD is the ACTUAL total incurred, which overshoots the cap on the
        // last item (see boosterFit) — you still have to wait that overshoot out.
        const boosterCD = edvd * EDVD_CD + fhc * FHC_CD + cans * 2 + candies * CANDY_CD_HRS;
        const cdCap     = 24 + c.voracity;
        const drugCD    = XAN_CD_AVG * xan;
        const cycleH    = Math.max(drugCD, boosterCD) + (cfg.refill ? c.cap / c.natE : 0);
        return {
            gain: sb.totalGain + fb.totalGain + rb.totalGain,
            happy, energy: stackE + fhc * c.cap + (cfg.refill ? c.cap : 0),
            trains: stackTr + fhcTr, boosterCD, drugCD, cdCap, cycleH: Math.max(cycleH, 1),
            // Feasible if everything BUT the final (overshooting) item fits under
            // the cap. Take the largest item as the last one consumed to be fair.
            boosterOverflow: boosterCD - Math.max(edvd?EDVD_CD:0, fhc?FHC_CD:0, cans?2:0, candies?CANDY_CD_HRS:0) > cdCap + 1e-9,
        };
    }

    // ── The ladder: free → full eDVD jump ────────────────────────────────────
    // The cost lever on a jump is the eDVD count, so the rungs scale that.
    //
    // Candy can't replace an eDVD stack, and that limit is structural rather than
    // about price: the booster cooldown pool is 24h (+Voracity), eDVD buys 417
    // happy per CD-hour and a 50-happy candy buys 100. Spend the whole 24h window
    // on candy and you get 2,400 happy; spend it on 4 eDVD and you get 10,000.
    // So candy is only ever a top-up in whatever booster room the eDVDs leave.
    //
    // Whether that top-up is worth buying IS purely a price question, and it's
    // decided per-run from the live market in evalTiers() — not hardcoded here.
    // For reference, a 50-happy candy beats an eDVD on $/happy below roughly
    // eDVD_price/50 (~$50k at a $2.5m eDVD), but that is NOT the gate: see below.
    function buildTiers(c) {
        const cdCap     = 24 + c.voracity;
        const edvdHappy = c.anJob ? EDVD_HAPPY * 2 : EDVD_HAPPY;
        const edvdPrice = allItemPrices.edvd || gf(el.hjEDVDCost) || 0;
        const edvdPerH  = edvdPrice > 0 ? edvdPrice / edvdHappy : Infinity;
        // Every candy burns the same 30min of booster cooldown, so once the window
        // is the binding limit it's happy-PER-ITEM that decides what the window is
        // worth — 49 slots yield 1,225 happy of Mints but 3,675 of Bloody Eyeballs.
        // Picking purely by cheapest-$/happy (as this used to) always lands on the
        // 25-happy sweets and quietly wastes most of the cooldown.
        // Rule: never pay more per happy than an eDVD does, and within that bound
        // take the densest candy available. That's the band the published methods
        // actually use (their "36 chocolates = 2,700 happy" is a 75-happy item).
        const candy = pickCandy(c, edvdPerH);
        const candyPerH = candy ? candy.price / (candy.happy * c.candyMult) : Infinity;

        const tiers = [
            { id:"nat",  name:"Natural regen only",     kind:"daily", refill:false, note:"Free. The floor everything else is measured against." },
            { id:"natR", name:"Natural regen + refill", kind:"daily", refill:true,  note:"One points refill a day, no drugs. Cheapest way to buy gains." },
            { id:"lsd6", name:"LSD grind — 6/day",      kind:"drug",  drug:"lsd",   doses:6, energyPer:50,  cdHrs:3, refill:false,
              note:"Cheapest energy in the game per $ and it never ODs. Short 3h cooldown means more, smaller doses." },
            { id:"xan2", name:"Xanax grind — 2/day",    kind:"drug",  drug:"xanax", doses:2, energyPer:250, cdHrs:XAN_CD_AVG, refill:false,
              note:"Two Xanax a day, train at cap. Low effort, no happy boosters." },
            { id:"xan3", name:"Xanax grind — 3/day",    kind:"drug",  drug:"xanax", doses:3, energyPer:250, cdHrs:XAN_CD_AVG, refill:true,
              note:"The workhorse method for most mid-tier players." },
        ];
        // Rung shapes follow what the community guides actually run. The pure
        // candy jump is first because Torn Intel's efficiency table puts the
        // chocolate methods top (2.39 and 1.75 %/$m) and the 5-eDVD stacks last
        // (0.97 and 0.66) — eDVD buys the biggest ABSOLUTE gain and the worst
        // value per dollar. maxEdvd is 5 at a base 24h cap, 9 at a 48h cap.
        const maxEdvd = boosterFit(cdCap, EDVD_CD);
        const rungs = [
            { id:"jCandy", name:"Candy jump — no eDVD",  xanax:4, edvd:0, fhc:0, refill:false,
              note:"The cheapest real jump and the best value per dollar in every published comparison. 49 candy + ecstasy." },
            { id:"j1dvd",  name:"Entry jump — 1 eDVD",   xanax:2, edvd:1, fhc:0, refill:false,
              note:"One eDVD plus a candy fill. The first rung where eDVD earns its place." },
            { id:"jHalf",  name:`Mixed jump — ${Math.max(1,Math.floor(maxEdvd/2))} eDVD + candy`, xanax:3, edvd:Math.max(1,Math.floor(maxEdvd/2)), fhc:0, refill:false,
              note:"Half an eDVD stack with candy filling the rest of the booster window. The efficiency sweet spot." },
            { id:"jFull",  name:`Full jump — ${maxEdvd} eDVD`, xanax:4, edvd:maxEdvd, fhc:0, refill:true,
              note:`The classic stack. ${maxEdvd} eDVD is the most your ${cdCap}h booster cap allows.` },
            { id:"jMax",   name:`Max jump — ${maxEdvd} eDVD + FHC`, xanax:4, edvd:maxEdvd, fhc:1, refill:true,
              note:"Everything on, including a Feathery Hotel Coupon for a second full energy bar." },
        ];
        for (const r of rungs) {
            // Candy's real price is cooldown, not cash. The cycle is
            // max(drugCD, boosterCD), so booster room sitting BELOW the drug
            // cooldown is free time — candy dropped there costs only its (tiny)
            // cash price. Past that, every candy stretches the cycle and cuts
            // jumps per week, so cap the fill at that free room.
            const drugCD   = XAN_CD_AVG * r.xanax;
            const usedCD   = r.edvd * EDVD_CD + r.fhc * FHC_CD;
            const freeRoom = Math.max(0, Math.min(cdCap, drugCD) - usedCD);
            const candies  = candy ? Math.min(boosterFit(cdCap, CANDY_CD_HRS, usedCD),
                                              boosterFit(freeRoom, CANDY_CD_HRS)) : 0;
            tiers.push({ ...r, kind:"jump", ecstasy:true, candies, candyType:candy, candyPerH, edvdPerH, freeRoom });
        }
        return tiers;
    }

    // ── Cost a tier two ways: full market price, and the cash gap after inventory ──
    function tierCost(t, c, p) {
        const need = [];
        const px = {
            xanax:   priceOf(p, ITEM_IDS.xanax,   allItemPrices.xanax,   el.hjXanaxCost),
            edvd:    priceOf(p, ITEM_IDS.edvd,    allItemPrices.edvd,    el.hjEDVDCost),
            ecstasy: priceOf(p, ITEM_IDS.ecstasy, allItemPrices.ecstasy, el.hjEcstasyCost),
            fhc:     priceOf(p, ITEM_IDS.fhc,     allItemPrices.fhc,     el.hjFHCCost),
            lsd:     priceOf(p, ITEM_IDS.lsd,     allItemPrices.lsd,     el.hjLSDCost),
        };
        const push = (label, id, name, qty, price) => {
            if (qty > 0) need.push({ label, id, name, qty, price, owned: invQty(p, id, name) });
        };
        if (t.kind === "drug") {
            if (t.drug === "lsd") push("LSD",   ITEM_IDS.lsd,   "LSD",   t.doses, px.lsd);
            else                  push("Xanax", ITEM_IDS.xanax, "Xanax", t.doses, px.xanax);
        }
        if (t.kind === "jump") {
            push("Xanax",   ITEM_IDS.xanax,   "Xanax",              t.xanax,   px.xanax);
            push("eDVD",    ITEM_IDS.edvd,    "Erotic DVD",         t.edvd,    px.edvd);
            push("Ecstasy", ITEM_IDS.ecstasy, "Ecstasy",            t.ecstasy?1:0, px.ecstasy);
            push("FHC",     ITEM_IDS.fhc,     "Feathery Hotel Coupon", t.fhc,  px.fhc);
            if (t.candies > 0 && t.candyType)
                push(t.candyType.label, t.candyType.id, t.candyType.name, t.candies, t.candyType.price);
        }

        let full = 0, gap = 0, unpriced = false;
        const shopping = [];
        for (const n of need) {
            if (!n.price) unpriced = true;
            full += n.price * n.qty;
            // owned === null means we couldn't read inventory — assume nothing owned
            const owned = n.owned == null ? 0 : n.owned;
            const short = Math.max(0, n.qty - owned);
            gap += n.price * short;
            shopping.push({ ...n, owned, short, cost: n.price * short });
        }

        // Points refill is bought with points, not items
        let refillCash = 0, refillOwned = false;
        if (t.refill) {
            refillOwned = p?.hasMoney ? p.points >= REFILL_POINTS : false;
            refillCash  = gf(el.dailyRefillCost) || 1725000;
            full += refillCash;
            if (!refillOwned) gap += refillCash;
        }
        return { need: shopping, full, gap, unpriced, refillCash, refillOwned };
    }

    // ── Evaluate every tier ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // The candy decision lives here because it needs both sims to answer honestly.
    // The test is NOT "is candy better value than an eDVD" — a player sitting on
    // the 1-eDVD rung is there precisely because more eDVDs are out of reach, so
    // benchmarking candy against eDVD would deny them the top-up exactly when it
    // might be the only happy they can afford. Instead: does the marginal stat
    // per $ of adding candy hold up against the stat per $ of the jump itself?
    // If yes it earns its place; if no it's just diluting the same money.
    function evalTiers(c, p) {
        return buildTiers(c).map(t => {
            let sim, cost;
            if      (t.kind === "daily") { sim = simDailyRegen(c, t.refill); cost = tierCost(t, c, p); }
            else if (t.kind === "drug")  { sim = simDrugDay(c, t.doses, t.energyPer, t.cdHrs, t.refill); cost = tierCost(t, c, p); }
            else {
                // Compare on WEEKLY figures, not per-jump. A per-jump comparison
                // misses that extra booster cooldown lengthens the cycle and so
                // cuts jumps per week — candy can look cheap per jump while making
                // you strictly worse off over a week.
                const weekly = (s, cst) => { const cyc = 168 / s.cycleH; return { gain: s.gain*cyc, cost: cst.full*cyc }; };
                const bare  = { ...t, candies: 0 };
                const simB  = simJump(c, bare), costB = tierCost(bare, c, p), wkB = weekly(simB, costB);
                if (t.candies > 0 && t.candyType) {
                    const simC  = simJump(c, t), costC = tierCost(t, c, p), wkC = weekly(simC, costC);
                    const dGain = wkC.gain - wkB.gain;
                    const dCost = wkC.cost - wkB.cost;
                    const baseEff = wkB.cost > 0 ? wkB.gain / (wkB.cost / 1e6) : Infinity;
                    const margEff = dCost > 0 ? dGain / (dCost / 1e6) : (dGain > 0 ? Infinity : -Infinity);
                    t.candyBaseEff = baseEff; t.candyMargEff = margEff;
                    t.candyCycleCost = simC.cycleH - simB.cycleH;
                    // Must actually gain more per week, and earn its keep doing it.
                    if (dGain > 0 && margEff >= baseEff) { sim = simC; cost = costC; t.candyKept = true; }
                    else { t.candyDropped = t.candies; t.candies = 0; sim = simB; cost = costB; }
                } else { sim = simB; cost = costB; }
            }

            const cyclesPerWk  = 168 / sim.cycleH;
            const weeklyGain   = sim.gain * cyclesPerWk;
            const weeklyCost   = cost.full * cyclesPerWk;
            // Stat per $1m spent — the honest efficiency number
            const perMillion   = weeklyCost > 0 ? weeklyGain / (weeklyCost / 1e6) : Infinity;
            return { t, sim, cost, cyclesPerWk, weeklyGain, weeklyCost, perMillion };
        });
    }

    // ── The recommender ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // sustainableWeekly is the budget line. Everything at or under it is fair game;
    // pick the biggest gains under the line. If nothing fits, fall back to free.
    function recommend(evals, p, income) {
        const reserve   = gf(el.assessReserve) || 5e6;
        const spendPct  = (gi(el.assessSpendPct) || 70) / 100;
        const cashNow   = p.hasMoney ? p.onhand + p.vault + (p.bankLeft === 0 ? p.bank : 0) : 0;
        const spendable = Math.max(0, cashNow - reserve);
        const sustainableWeekly = income.weekly != null && income.weekly > 0
            ? income.weekly * spendPct
            : spendable / 4;   // no income signal: spread the spendable cash over a month

        for (const e of evals) {
            e.affordable   = e.weeklyCost <= sustainableWeekly;
            // Can they run this tier right now, from stock, without waiting on a CD?
            const drugBlocked    = (e.t.kind !== "daily") && p.cdDrug > 0;
            const boosterBlocked = e.t.kind === "jump" && e.sim.boosterCD > 0 && p.cdBooster > 0;
            e.blockers = [];
            if (!p.hasInv)                 e.blockers.push("inventory unknown");
            if (drugBlocked)               e.blockers.push(`drug CD ${fmtDur(p.cdDrug)}`);
            if (boosterBlocked)            e.blockers.push(`booster CD ${fmtDur(p.cdBooster)}`);
            if (e.cost.gap > cashNow)      e.blockers.push(`short $${fmt(e.cost.gap - cashNow)}`);
            if (e.sim.boosterOverflow)     e.blockers.push(`needs ${fmtD(e.sim.boosterCD,0)}h booster CD, cap is ${e.sim.cdCap}h`);
            if (e.t.refill && p.refillUsed) e.blockers.push("refill already used today");
            e.readyNow = e.blockers.length === 0 && p.hasInv;
        }

        const viable    = evals.filter(e => !e.sim.boosterOverflow);
        const inBudget  = viable.filter(e => e.affordable);
        const primary   = (inBudget.length ? inBudget : viable.filter(e => e.weeklyCost === 0))
                            .reduce((a, b) => (b.weeklyGain > a.weeklyGain ? b : a), inBudget[0] || viable[0]);
        const bestValue = viable.filter(e => isFinite(e.perMillion))
                            .reduce((a, b) => (!a || b.perMillion > a.perMillion ? b : a), null);
        const dream     = viable.reduce((a, b) => (b.weeklyGain > a.weeklyGain ? b : a), viable[0]);
        const readyNow  = viable.filter(e => e.readyNow)
                            .reduce((a, b) => (!a || b.weeklyGain > a.weeklyGain ? b : a), null);
        // The actionable upgrade: cheapest tier that beats the pick by a real margin.
        // "Save up for the max jump" is useless advice to someone on natural regen —
        // the next rung up is what they can actually work toward.
        const nextStep = viable
            .filter(e => e !== primary && e.weeklyGain > primary.weeklyGain * 1.15)
            .sort((a, b) => a.weeklyCost - b.weeklyCost)[0] || null;
        return { primary, bestValue, dream, nextStep, readyNow, cashNow, spendable, sustainableWeekly, reserve, spendPct };
    }

    // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    // Which path is this player already closest to? Scores each by how little the
    // whored stat would have to move, so someone who never picked a path still
    // gets a concrete "you're nearly there on this one" instead of a shrug.
    // Per-stat allowances for the chosen path. "The limit" means something
    // different depending on the job each stat is doing, which is exactly what
    // makes these builds confusing — so spell it out per stat:
    //   whored — needs a FLOOR (solo gym wants it 25% clear of 2nd highest) and
    //            has a CEILING (push too far and the pair gym's sum test fails)
    //   middle — has a CEILING: grow past whore/1.25 and this stat becomes the
    //            new "second highest", which shuts the solo gym
    //   forget — has a CEILING from the pair gym: whore+forget must stay small
    //            enough that the two middle stats clear it by 25%
    // Per-stat headroom, following RGiskard's branch logic exactly.
    //   combo gym  — its two stats must SUM to 25% above the other two's sum
    //   solo gym   — its stat must be 25% above your second-highest stat
    // Which limit binds depends on the role a stat plays in the chosen build, and a
    // stat that's in neither gym is bound by BOTH (take the tighter one).
    function statLimits(pathKey, stats) {
        const P = BUILD_PATHS[pathKey]; if (!P) return null;
        const C = COMBO_GYMS[P.combo];
        // RGiskard's flat 1m warning buffer is right for a big account and useless
        // for a small one, so scale it to 5% of total stats with a 1m ceiling.
        const totalStat = Object.keys(STAT_KEYS).reduce((s, k) => s + stats[k], 0);
        const safeDist  = Math.min(STAT_SAFE_DISTANCE, Math.max(1000, totalStat * 0.05));
        const comboPrimarySum = stats[C.stat1] + stats[C.stat2];
        const comboSecondSum  = stats[C.sec1]  + stats[C.sec2];
        const distFromComboMin = comboSecondSum * RATIO_MIN - comboPrimarySum; // >0 → primaries too low
        const distToComboMax   = comboPrimarySum / RATIO_MIN - comboSecondSum; // <0 → secondaries too high

        let minPrimaryStat = 0, maxSecondaryStat = 0;
        if (P.whore) {
            const highestOther = Math.max(...Object.keys(STAT_KEYS).filter(k => k !== P.whore).map(k => stats[k]));
            minPrimaryStat   = highestOther * RATIO_MIN;
            maxSecondaryStat = stats[P.whore] / RATIO_MIN;
        }

        const comboGym = GYM_BY_ID[C.id];
        const soloGym  = P.whore ? GYM_BY_ID[SOLO_GYM_FOR[P.whore]] : null;
        const pairName = `${STAT_ABBREV[C.stat1]}+${STAT_ABBREV[C.stat2]}`;
        const out = [];

        for (const key of Object.keys(STAT_KEYS)) {
            const v = stats[key];
            const isWhore = key === P.whore;
            const isComboPrimary = key === C.stat1 || key === C.stat2;
            let role, min = null, max = null, why, headroom;

            // `bound` decides the wording: a FLOOR you want to be above, a CEILING
            // you want to be below. headroom is always "distance to that limit,
            // positive = safe", so the sign never has to mean two different things.
            let bound = "ceiling";
            if (isWhore) {
                role = "whoring this";
                min  = minPrimaryStat;
                why  = `Must be 25% above your next-highest stat to hold ${soloGym?.name || "the solo gym"}.`;
                if (v < minPrimaryStat) { bound = "floor"; headroom = v - minPrimaryStat; max = null; }
                else if (P.ratio === "Baldr") {
                    // Whored stat is also a combo primary — a bigger value helps BOTH
                    // gyms, so the floor is the only limit and being over it is good.
                    bound = "floor"; headroom = v - minPrimaryStat; max = null;
                    why += ` It's also half of ${comboGym?.name || "the combo gym"}, so more is purely good — no upper limit.`;
                } else {
                    // Hank's: whored stat is a combo SECONDARY, so the combo gym caps it.
                    max = v + distToComboMax; headroom = distToComboMax;
                    why += ` But ${pairName} must beat it by 25% for ${comboGym?.name}, so it's capped too.`;
                }
            } else if (isComboPrimary) {
                role = "combo partner";
                max  = maxSecondaryStat || null;
                headroom = P.whore ? maxSecondaryStat - v : distToComboMax;
                why  = P.whore
                    ? `Past ${fmt(maxSecondaryStat)} it becomes your second-highest stat and ${soloGym?.name} closes.`
                    : `Half of ${comboGym?.name} — this pair must stay 25% above the other two.`;
            } else {
                role = P.ratio === "Combo" ? "keep low" : "leave behind";
                // Bound by the combo gym AND (if there's a solo gym) by not becoming
                // the second-highest stat. Whichever bites first is the real limit.
                const toComboMax = distToComboMax;
                const toSoloMax  = P.whore ? maxSecondaryStat - v : Infinity;
                headroom = Math.min(toComboMax, toSoloMax);
                max = v + headroom;
                why = toComboMax <= toSoloMax
                    ? `${pairName} must beat ${STAT_ABBREV[C.sec1]}+${STAT_ABBREV[C.sec2]} by 25% for ${comboGym?.name}.`
                    : `Past ${fmt(maxSecondaryStat)} it becomes your second-highest stat and ${soloGym?.name} closes.`;
            }

            const tooLow  = bound === "floor"   && headroom < 0;
            const tooHigh = bound === "ceiling" && headroom < 0;
            // Wording matched to RGiskard's: "too low!" / "too high!" when breached,
            // "above the limit" (floor) / "below the limit" (ceiling) when safe.
            const verdict = tooLow  ? `${STAT_LABELS[key]} is ${fmtK(headroom)} too low!`
                          : tooHigh ? `${STAT_LABELS[key]} is ${fmtK(headroom)} too high!`
                          : bound === "floor" ? `${STAT_LABELS[key]} is ${fmtK(headroom)} above the limit.`
                          :                     `${STAT_LABELS[key]} is ${fmtK(headroom)} below the limit.`;
            out.push({ key, role, min, max, headroom, why, tooLow, tooHigh, bound, verdict,
                       ok: !tooLow && !tooHigh,
                       near: !tooLow && !tooHigh && headroom < safeDist });
        }
        return { rows: out, comboGym, soloGym, distFromComboMin, distToComboMax,
                 comboOk: distFromComboMin <= 0,
                 soloOk: P.whore ? stats[P.whore] >= minPrimaryStat : true, P };
    }
    // 145,100 -> "145.1k"; keeps the limit lines short enough to read at a glance

    function suggestPath(stats) {
        const maxS = Math.max(...Object.keys(STAT_KEYS).map(k => stats[k]), 1);
        let best = null;
        for (const key of Object.keys(BUILD_PATHS)) {
            const A = analysePath(key, stats);
            if (!A) continue;
            const open = (A.soloOk ? 1 : 0) + (A.pairOk ? 1 : 0);
            // Fit matters more than what happens to be open. The stat you'd whore
            // should already lead; the one you'd abandon should already be your
            // smallest. Telling someone to forget a stat they've spent months
            // building is not a real option, however many gyms it lights up.
            const fit   = (stats[A.P.whore] - A.forgetSum / Math.max(A.P.forget.length, 1)) / maxS;
            const score = (A.feasible ? 2 : 0) + fit * 2 + open * 0.5;
            if (!best || score > best.score) best = { key, A, score, open, fit };
        }
        return best;
    }

    function analysePath(pathKey, stats) {
        const P0 = BUILD_PATHS[pathKey];
        if (!P0) return null;
        // Derive the roles from the combo gym rather than hardcoding them, so this
        // works for Hank's (1 whore / 2 middles / 1 abandoned), Baldr's (1 whore /
        // 1 partner / 2 abandoned) and the combo-only builds alike.
        const C   = COMBO_GYMS[P0.combo];
        const all = Object.keys(STAT_KEYS);
        const mid = [C.stat1, C.stat2].filter(k => k !== P0.whore);
        const P = { ...P0, mid,
                    forget: all.filter(k => k !== P0.whore && !mid.includes(k)),
                    solo: P0.whore ? SOLO_GYM_FOR[P0.whore] : null,
                    pair: C.id };
        if (!P.whore) {
            // Combo-only build: no solo gym, so most of the band maths doesn't apply.
            const primarySum = stats[C.stat1] + stats[C.stat2];
            const secondSum  = stats[C.sec1]  + stats[C.sec2];
            return { P, ratio: secondSum > 0 ? primarySum / secondSum : 0,
                     soloOk: true, pairOk: primarySum >= secondSum * RATIO_MIN,
                     needWhore: 0, maxWhore: Infinity, feasible: true,
                     midAvg: primarySum / 2, midSum: primarySum,
                     soloGym: null, pairGym: GYM_BY_ID[C.id], inBand: true };
        }
        const midAvg = P.mid.reduce((s, k) => s + stats[k], 0) / Math.max(P.mid.length, 1);
        const ratio  = midAvg > 0 ? stats[P.whore] / midAvg : 0;
        const soloOk = SPECIALIST_REQS[P.solo]?.check(stats) ?? false;
        const pairOk = SPECIALIST_REQS[P.pair]?.check(stats) ?? false;
        // What the whored stat needs to be for the solo gym, given the others
        const others   = Object.keys(STAT_KEYS).filter(k => k !== P.whore).map(k => stats[k]);
        const needWhore = Math.max(...others) * RATIO_MIN;
        // Ceiling before the pair gym breaks: pairSum >= 1.25 x (whore + forget)
        const pairSum   = P.mid.reduce((s, k) => s + stats[k], 0);
        const forgetSum = P.forget.reduce((s, k) => s + stats[k], 0);
        const maxWhore  = pairSum / RATIO_MIN - forgetSum;
        // The two gyms pull in opposite directions: the solo gym wants the whored
        // stat HIGH, the pair gym wants whore+forget LOW. If the forgotten stat is
        // already large, the floor can sit above the ceiling and no value of the
        // whored stat satisfies both — the path is shut until the middle stats grow.
        const feasible  = maxWhore >= needWhore;
        // Combined middle-stat total that would reopen the band.
        const midNeeded = (needWhore + forgetSum) * RATIO_MIN;
        return { P, ratio, soloOk, pairOk, needWhore, maxWhore, midAvg, feasible,
                 midNeeded, midSum: pairSum, forgetSum,
                 soloGym: GYM_BY_ID[P.solo], pairGym: GYM_BY_ID[P.pair],
                 inBand: ratio >= RATIO_MIN && ratio <= RATIO_MAX };
    }

    // ── eDVD trade-off sweep ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // The question this answers: "I own 3 eDVDs. Do I buy 3 more, or fill the
    // rest of the booster window with candy and jump now?" It walks every eDVD
    // count your cap allows, tops each one up with candy, and prices the shortfall
    // against what's actually in your inventory — so you can see exactly what the
    // extra eDVDs buy you and decide whether the gain is worth the cash.
    function edvdTradeoff(c, p) {
        // Same candy rule as buildTiers, or this table would contradict the ladder.
        const edvdHappy = c.anJob ? EDVD_HAPPY * 2 : EDVD_HAPPY;
        const edvdPrice = allItemPrices.edvd || gf(el.hjEDVDCost) || 0;
        const candy   = pickCandy(c, edvdPrice > 0 ? edvdPrice / edvdHappy : Infinity);
        const cdCap   = 24 + c.voracity;
        const maxEdvd = boosterFit(cdCap, EDVD_CD);
        const owned   = invQty(p, ITEM_IDS.edvd, "Erotic DVD");
        const rows    = [];
        for (let e = 0; e <= maxEdvd; e++) {
            const usedCD   = e * EDVD_CD;
            const drugCD   = XAN_CD_AVG * 4;
            const freeRoom = Math.max(0, Math.min(cdCap, drugCD) - usedCD);
            const candies  = candy ? Math.min(boosterFit(cdCap, CANDY_CD_HRS, usedCD),
                                              boosterFit(freeRoom, CANDY_CD_HRS)) : 0;
            const cfg = { kind:"jump", xanax:4, edvd:e, fhc:0, refill:false, ecstasy:true, candies, candyType:candy };
            const sim = simJump(c, cfg);
            if (sim.boosterOverflow) continue;
            const cost = tierCost(cfg, c, p);
            const perWk = 168 / sim.cycleH;
            rows.push({ e, candies, sim, cost, gain: sim.gain,
                        weeklyGain: sim.gain * perWk, weeklyCost: cost.full * perWk,
                        buy: Math.max(0, e - (owned ?? 0)) });
        }
        return { rows, owned, maxEdvd, candy, cdCap };
    }

    const fmtDur = s => s <= 0 ? "ready" : s >= 3600 ? `${Math.floor(s/3600)}h ${Math.round(s%3600/60)}m` : `${Math.round(s/60)}m`;
    const fmtBig = v => !isFinite(v) ? "—" : v >= 1e9 ? `$${(v/1e9).toFixed(2)}b` : v >= 1e6 ? `$${(v/1e6).toFixed(1)}m` : `$${fmt(v)}`;

    // ── Render ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    async function runAssessment() {
        const out = el.assessOut;
        if (!out) return;
        out.innerHTML = `<div style="font-size:12px;color:#667;padding:8px 0">⟳ Pulling your data…</div>`;

        let c = assessCtx();
        if (!c.gym)  { out.innerHTML = `<div class="gg-status warn" style="display:block">⚠ Select your gym first.</div>`; return; }
        if (!c.dots) { out.innerHTML = `<div class="gg-status warn" style="display:block">⚠ ${c.gym.name} doesn't train ${c.statLabel}.</div>`; return; }
        if (!c.statNow) { out.innerHTML = `<div class="gg-status warn" style="display:block">⚠ Enter your current stat (or hit ⟳ Auto-fill) first.</div>`; return; }

        // Prices are required to cost the tiers — fetch if the cache is cold
        if (!allItemPrices.xanax || Object.keys(candyPrices).length === 0) { try { await fetchPrices(false); } catch(_) {} }

        const p = await fetchPlayerData();
        if (p.error) { out.innerHTML = `<div class="gg-status warn" style="display:block">⚠ ${p.error}</div>`; return; }
        player = p;

        // Push a detected Voracity into the shared field and rebuild the context,
        // so the booster cap is right here AND in the Happy Jump tab from now on.
        if (p.voracityHrs != null && el.hjVoracity && gi(el.hjVoracity) !== p.voracityHrs) {
            el.hjVoracity.value = String(p.voracityHrs);
            gg_save("hjVoracity", String(p.voracityHrs));
            c = assessCtx();
        }

        const income = estimateIncome(p);
        const evals  = evalTiers(c, p);
        const rec    = recommend(evals, p, income);

        let html = "";

        // ── Key-access warning ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        if (p.limited) {
            html += `<div style="padding:9px 11px;background:#1e1a10;border:1px solid #4a3a18;border-radius:4px;font-size:11px;color:#bf9f5a;line-height:1.6;margin-bottom:10px">
  ⚠ <strong>Limited key</strong> — no money, net worth or inventory. Affordability below is guesswork.<br>
  <a href="https://www.torn.com/preferences.php#tab=api?step=addNewKey&title=AIO+Planner+Full&type=4" target="_blank" style="color:#7a9aca;text-decoration:none">🔑 Create a Full Access key →</a> then paste it in ⚙ Settings.
</div>`;
        }

        // ── Money ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        html += `<div class="gg-tldr">
  <div class="gg-tldr-title">💰 Where you stand</div>
  <div class="gg-tldr-grid">
    <div class="gg-tldr-cell"><div class="gg-tldr-val">${p.hasMoney?fmtBig(rec.cashNow):"—"}</div><div class="gg-tldr-lbl">cash available</div></div>
    <div class="gg-tldr-cell"><div class="gg-tldr-val">${income.weekly!=null?fmtBig(income.weekly):"—"}</div><div class="gg-tldr-lbl">est. income / week</div></div>
    <div class="gg-tldr-cell"><div class="gg-tldr-val">${fmtBig(rec.sustainableWeekly)}</div><div class="gg-tldr-lbl">training budget / week</div></div>
    <div class="gg-tldr-cell"><div class="gg-tldr-val">${p.networth?fmtBig(p.networth):"—"}</div><div class="gg-tldr-lbl">net worth</div></div>
  </div>
</div>`;

        if (p.hasMoney) {
            html += `<details class="gg-dis"><summary>Money &mdash; ${fmtBig(rec.cashNow)} available, ${fmtBig(rec.spendable)} spendable</summary><div class="inner">`;
            html += row("On hand",  `$${fmt(p.onhand)}`);
            if (p.vault > 0) html += row("Vault", `$${fmt(p.vault)}`);
            if (p.bank  > 0) html += row("City bank", `$${fmt(p.bank)}${p.bankLeft>0?` — locked ${fmtDur(p.bankLeft)}, excluded`:" — available"}`, p.bankLeft>0?"a":"");
            html += row("Points", `${fmt(p.points)} (${p.points>=REFILL_POINTS?`${Math.floor(p.points/REFILL_POINTS)} refills in hand`:`need ${REFILL_POINTS} for a refill`})`, p.points>=REFILL_POINTS?"g":"a");
            html += row("Reserve kept back", `$${fmt(rec.reserve)} — spendable: $${fmt(rec.spendable)}`, "b");
            html += `</div></details>`;
        }

        html += `<details class="gg-dis"><summary>Income &mdash; ${income.weekly != null ? fmtBig(income.weekly) + "/week, " + income.conf + " confidence" : "unknown"}</summary><div class="inner">`;
        html += row("Estimate", income.weekly != null
            ? `${fmtBig(income.weekly)}/week — ${income.source}`
            : "Unknown — log a few days, or type your weekly income above");
        html += row("Confidence", {
            manual:"Your own figure — used as-is",
            high:"High — 2+ weeks of tracked net worth",
            medium:"Medium — several days tracked",
            low:"Low — under 5 days tracked, will sharpen",
            rough:"Rough — lifetime average, ignores recent activity",
            none:"None yet",
        }[income.conf] || "—", income.conf==="high"?"g":income.conf==="none"?"r":"a");
        if (p.rentWeekly > 0) html += row("Property rent", `${fmtBig(p.rentWeekly)}/week (included in net worth growth)`);
        if (!income.tracked) html += row("Tip", "Open this panel every day or two — net worth snapshots build a real income figure.", "b");
        html += `</div></details>`;

        // ── Readiness ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        const blockers = [];
        if (p.cdDrug > 0)     blockers.push("drug " + fmtDur(p.cdDrug));
        if (p.cdBooster > 0)  blockers.push("booster " + fmtDur(p.cdBooster));
        if (p.refillUsed)     blockers.push("refill used");
        html += `<div class="gg-note ${blockers.length ? "warn" : "ok"}" style="line-height:1.6">
  <strong>${blockers.length ? "Partly blocked" : "Ready to train"}</strong>${blockers.length ? " &mdash; " + blockers.join(" &#183; ") : " &mdash; nothing on cooldown"}
</div>`;
        html += `<details class="gg-dis"><summary>Cooldowns and capacity</summary><div class="inner">`;
        html += row("Drug cooldown",    fmtDur(p.cdDrug),    p.cdDrug===0?"g":"a");
        // Booster capacity is dynamic twice over: the CAP moves with Voracity, and
        // what's usable right now is the cap minus whatever cooldown is already
        // burnt. Both change the size of jump you can actually build today.
        const capHrs   = 24 + c.voracity;
        const burntHrs = p.cdBooster / 3600;
        const roomHrs  = Math.max(0, capHrs - burntHrs);
        html += row("Booster cooldown", `${fmtDur(p.cdBooster)} of ${capHrs}h cap — ${fmtD(roomHrs,1)}h room`, p.cdBooster===0?"g":"a");
        html += row("Max booster cap", c.voracity > 0
            ? `${capHrs}h (24h base + ${c.voracity}h Voracity) → up to ${boosterFit(capHrs, EDVD_CD)} eDVD per jump`
            : `24h, no Voracity detected → up to ${boosterFit(24, EDVD_CD)} eDVD per jump`, c.voracity > 0 ? "g" : "");
        if (p.voracitySrc) {
            html += row("↳ from perk", `"${p.voracitySrc.replace(/[<>]/g,'')}" — auto-read from your faction perks`, "b");
        } else if (!p.perkListSeen || c.voracity === 0) {
            html += row("↳ Voracity", `Not found in your perks. If your faction has it, set "Voracity hrs" in 📋 Long-term plan → Happy Jumps and every calculation here follows.`, "a");
        }
        if (p.cdBooster > 0) {
            html += row("Fits right now", `${boosterFit(roomHrs, EDVD_CD)} eDVD or ${boosterFit(roomHrs, CANDY_CD_HRS)} candy until the rest clears`, "a");
        }
        html += row("Points refill",    p.refillUsed ? "Already used today" : "Available", p.refillUsed?"a":"g");
        if (p.energyNow != null) html += row("Energy", `${p.energyNow}/${p.energyMax}`);
        if (p.overdosed > 0 && (p.xanTaken + p.extTaken) > 20) {
            const odRate = p.overdosed / (p.xanTaken + p.extTaken) * 100;
            html += row("Your real OD rate", `${odRate.toFixed(2)}% over ${fmt(p.xanTaken+p.extTaken)} drugs (${p.overdosed} ODs) — beats the community guess`, "b");
        }
        if (p.xanTaken + p.extTaken <= 50 && p.xanTaken + p.extTaken > 0) {
            html += row("⚠ Sports Science Lab", `${p.xanTaken} Xanax + ${p.extTaken} Ecstasy of 50 lifetime — jumping burns this window`, "a");
        }

        html += `</div></details>`;
        // ── Ladder ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        html += rsec("Your options, cheapest first");
        html += `<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1.5fr) 60px 62px 54px;gap:0 6px;margin-bottom:2px">
  <div style="font-size:9px;color:#445;text-transform:uppercase;border-bottom:1px solid #1e1e2a;padding-bottom:3px">Method</div>
  <div style="font-size:9px;color:#445;text-transform:uppercase;text-align:right;border-bottom:1px solid #1e1e2a;padding-bottom:3px">Gain/wk</div>
  <div style="font-size:9px;color:#445;text-transform:uppercase;text-align:right;border-bottom:1px solid #1e1e2a;padding-bottom:3px">Cost/wk</div>
  <div style="font-size:9px;color:#445;text-transform:uppercase;text-align:right;border-bottom:1px solid #1e1e2a;padding-bottom:3px">Stat/$m</div>
</div>`;
        for (const e of evals) {
            const isPick = e === rec.primary;
            const col    = e.sim.boosterOverflow ? "#664" : isPick ? "#7abf7a" : e.affordable ? "#aab" : "#665";
            const badges = [];
            if (isPick)                 badges.push(`<span style="font-size:9px;color:#3a7a3a;background:#182018;border:1px solid #2a4a2a;border-radius:2px;padding:0 3px">PICK</span>`);
            if (e === rec.bestValue && !isPick) badges.push(`<span style="font-size:9px;color:#7a7a3a;background:#1a1a10;border:1px solid #3a3a1a;border-radius:2px;padding:0 3px">VALUE</span>`);
            if (e.readyNow)             badges.push(`<span style="font-size:9px;color:#3a6a7a;background:#101a20;border:1px solid #1a3a4a;border-radius:2px;padding:0 3px">${e.cost.gap===0?"IN STOCK":"CAN DO NOW"}</span>`);
            if (!e.affordable && e.weeklyCost > 0) badges.push(`<span style="font-size:9px;color:#7a4a3a">over budget</span>`);
            html += `<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1.5fr) 60px 62px 54px;gap:0 6px;padding:4px 0;border-bottom:1px solid #1a1a22;align-items:baseline">
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:${col};font-weight:${isPick?'bold':'normal'};line-height:1.4">${e.t.name} ${badges.join(" ")}</div>
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:${col};text-align:right">+${fmt(e.weeklyGain)}</div>
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:${e.weeklyCost===0?'#5a8a5a':col};text-align:right">${e.weeklyCost===0?"free":fmtBig(e.weeklyCost)}</div>
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:#778;text-align:right">${isFinite(e.perMillion)?fmt(e.perMillion):"∞"}</div>
</div>`;
        }

        // ── Recommendation ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        const R = rec.primary;
        html += `<div style="margin-top:12px;padding:12px;background:#10181c;border:1px solid #2a3a48;border-radius:6px">
  <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:bold;color:#9accbf;margin-bottom:6px">🎯 Run this: ${R.t.name}</div>
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:#778;line-height:1.6;margin-bottom:8px">${R.t.note}</div>
  <div style="font-size:12px;color:#aab;line-height:1.7">
    +${fmt(R.weeklyGain)} ${c.statLabel}/week · ${R.weeklyCost===0?"free":`${fmtBig(R.weeklyCost)}/week`}
    ${R.weeklyCost>0?` · ${((R.weeklyCost/Math.max(rec.sustainableWeekly,1))*100).toFixed(0)}% of your budget`:""}
  </div>`;
        if (rec.nextStep) {
            const need = rec.nextStep.weeklyCost / rec.spendPct;
            html += `<div style="margin-top:8px;padding-top:8px;border-top:1px solid #1e2a34;font-size:11px;color:#8a9aaa;line-height:1.6">
    ⬆ <strong style="color:#bf9f5a">Next step up: ${rec.nextStep.t.name}</strong> — +${fmt(rec.nextStep.weeklyGain)}/week
    (${((rec.nextStep.weeklyGain/Math.max(R.weeklyGain,1)-1)*100).toFixed(0)}% more) at ${fmtBig(rec.nextStep.weeklyCost)}/week.<br>
    Needs about <strong>${fmtBig(need)}/week income</strong> to sustain${income.weekly!=null&&income.weekly>0?` — ${(need/income.weekly).toFixed(1)}× what you make now`:""}.
  </div>`;
        }
        if (rec.dream && rec.dream !== R && rec.dream !== rec.nextStep) {
            html += `<div style="margin-top:6px;font-size:11px;color:#778;line-height:1.6">🏁 Ceiling: <strong>${rec.dream.t.name}</strong> at +${fmt(rec.dream.weeklyGain)}/week for ${fmtBig(rec.dream.weeklyCost)}/week.</div>`;
        }
        if (rec.bestValue && rec.bestValue !== R) {
            html += `<div style="margin-top:6px;font-size:11px;color:#8a8a6a">💡 Best raw value is <strong>${rec.bestValue.t.name}</strong> at ${fmt(rec.bestValue.perMillion)} ${c.statLabel} per $1m.</div>`;
        }
        if (R.t.candyKept) {
            html += `<div style="margin-top:6px;font-size:11px;color:#7a9a7a">🍬 ${R.t.candies}× ${R.t.candyType.label} at $${fmt(R.t.candyPerH)}/happy — this fits in the ${fmtD(R.t.freeRoom,1)}h of booster cooldown sitting under your ${fmtD(XAN_CD_AVG*R.t.xanax,0)}h drug cooldown, so it costs you no extra cycle time. Practically free happy.</div>`;
        } else if (R.t.candyDropped) {
            const why = R.t.candyCycleCost > 0
                ? `it would add ${fmtD(R.t.candyCycleCost,1)}h to every cycle, costing you more jumps per week than the happy is worth`
                : `it returns only ${fmt(R.t.candyMargEff)} ${c.statLabel} per $1m against this jump's ${fmt(R.t.candyBaseEff)}`;
            html += `<div style="margin-top:6px;font-size:11px;color:#8a8a6a">🍬 Candy left out — ${why}.</div>`;
        } else if (R.t.kind === "jump" && R.t.freeRoom === 0) {
            html += `<div style="margin-top:6px;font-size:11px;color:#8a8a6a">🍬 No candy — your eDVDs already fill the booster cooldown window.</div>`;
        }
        html += `</div>`;

        // ── Shopping list ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        html += rsec(`Shopping list — one ${R.t.kind === "jump" ? "jump" : "day"} of ${R.t.name}`);
        if (!R.cost.need.length && !R.t.refill) {
            html += `<div style="font-size:11px;color:#5a8a5a;padding:4px 0">Nothing to buy — this method is free.</div>`;
        } else {
            html += `<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1.3fr) 40px 40px 40px 66px;gap:0 6px;margin-bottom:2px">
  ${["Item","Need","Have","Buy","Cost"].map((h,i)=>`<div style="font-size:9px;color:#445;text-transform:uppercase;text-align:${i?'right':'left'};border-bottom:1px solid #1e1e2a;padding-bottom:3px">${h}</div>`).join("")}
</div>`;
            for (const n of R.cost.need) {
                const ok = n.short === 0;
                html += `<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1.3fr) 40px 40px 40px 66px;gap:0 6px;padding:4px 0;border-bottom:1px solid #1a1a22;align-items:baseline">
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:${ok?'#5a8a5a':'#aab'}">${ok?"✓ ":""}${n.label}</div>
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:#778;text-align:right">${n.qty}</div>
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:${ok?'#5a8a5a':'#778'};text-align:right">${p.hasInv?n.owned:"?"}</div>
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:${ok?'#445':'#bf9f5a'};text-align:right">${n.short||"—"}</div>
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:${ok?'#445':'#aab'};text-align:right">${n.short?fmtBig(n.cost):"—"}</div>
</div>`;
            }
            if (R.t.refill) {
                html += `<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1.3fr) 40px 40px 40px 66px;gap:0 6px;padding:4px 0;border-bottom:1px solid #1a1a22;align-items:baseline">
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:${R.cost.refillOwned?'#5a8a5a':'#aab'}">${R.cost.refillOwned?"✓ ":""}Points refill (${REFILL_POINTS}pts)</div>
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:#778;text-align:right">1</div>
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:#778;text-align:right">${p.hasMoney?Math.floor(p.points/REFILL_POINTS):"?"}</div>
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:${R.cost.refillOwned?'#445':'#bf9f5a'};text-align:right">${R.cost.refillOwned?"—":"1"}</div>
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:#aab;text-align:right">${R.cost.refillOwned?"—":fmtBig(R.cost.refillCash)}</div>
</div>`;
            }
            const affordGap = R.cost.gap <= rec.cashNow;
            html += `<div style="margin-top:8px;padding:8px 10px;border-radius:4px;background:${R.cost.gap===0?'#0a1a14':affordGap?'#101a20':'#1a1408'};border:1px solid ${R.cost.gap===0?'#1a3a28':affordGap?'#1a3a4a':'#3a2818'};font-size:12px;color:${R.cost.gap===0?'#7abf9a':affordGap?'#8abfcf':'#bf9f5a'};line-height:1.6">`;
            if (!p.hasInv) {
                html += `Full market cost if you own none of it: <strong>${fmtBig(R.cost.full)}</strong>.<br>
                <span style="font-size:10px">Can't see your inventory, so this is priced as if buying everything — check what you already have before spending. Reason: ${(p.invReason || "unknown").replace(/[<>]/g,'')}</span>`;
            } else if (R.cost.gap === 0) {
                html += `✓ <strong>You already own everything.</strong> Full market value ${fmtBig(R.cost.full)} — you can run this now for $0.`;
            } else if (affordGap) {
                html += `You need to buy <strong>${fmtBig(R.cost.gap)}</strong> worth (of ${fmtBig(R.cost.full)} full price). You have ${fmtBig(rec.cashNow)} — <strong>you can afford it.</strong>`;
            } else {
                html += `Short by <strong>${fmtBig(R.cost.gap - rec.cashNow)}</strong>. You need ${fmtBig(R.cost.gap)} to top up, have ${fmtBig(rec.cashNow)}.`;
            }
            html += `</div>`;
            if (R.cost.unpriced) html += `<div style="font-size:10px;color:#7a5a3a;margin-top:5px">⚠ Some items had no live price — hit ⟳ Live Prices in 💰 Item Costs for accurate totals.</div>`;
        }

        // ── Specialisation path ───────────────────────────────────────────────
        const pathKey  = el.buildPath?.value || "";
        const hasStats = allStats.strength > 0 || allStats.speed > 0 || allStats.defense > 0 || allStats.dexterity > 0;

        if (!hasStats) {
            html += rsec("Build path");
            html += row("⚠ Need your stats", "Hit ⟳ Auto-fill first so your ratio can be checked against real battle stats.", "a");
        } else {
            const chosen = pathKey || suggestPath(allStats)?.key;
            const A = chosen ? analysePath(chosen, allStats) : null;
            if (A) {
                const W = STAT_ABBREV[A.P.whore], F = A.P.forget.map(k=>STAT_ABBREV[k]).join("+");
                const M = A.P.mid.map(m => STAT_ABBREV[m]);
                const maxStat = Math.max(...Object.keys(STAT_KEYS).map(k => allStats[k]), 1);
                const floorV  = Math.round(A.needWhore);   // below this, solo gym shuts
                const ceilV   = Math.round(A.maxWhore);    // above this, pair gym shuts
                const cur     = allStats[A.P.whore];

                html += rsec(pathKey ? "Your build path" : "Build path — suggested (pick one above to lock it in)");

                // Plain-English header
                html += `<div style="padding:10px 12px;background:#12181c;border:1px solid #24323c;border-radius:5px;margin-bottom:8px">
  <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:bold;color:#9accbf;margin-bottom:3px">🎯 Whore ${STAT_LABELS[A.P.whore].toUpperCase()} · forget ${A.P.forget.map(k=>STAT_LABELS[k]).join(" + ").toUpperCase()}</div>
  <div style="font-size:10px;color:#5a7a6a;margin-bottom:4px">${A.P.ratio === "Baldr" ? "Baldr's Ratio — the solo gym shares a stat with the combo gym" : A.P.ratio === "Hank" ? "Hank's Ratio — the two gyms cover three different stats" : "Combo gym only"}</div>
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:#778;line-height:1.6">
    Push <strong>${STAT_LABELS[A.P.whore]}</strong> ahead. Grow <strong>${A.P.mid.map(m=>STAT_LABELS[m]).join(" and ")}</strong> together as your middle stats.
    Leave <strong>${A.P.forget.map(k=>STAT_LABELS[k]).join(" and ")}</strong> alone. That combination is what opens two specialist gyms at the same time.
  </div>
</div>`;

                // Per-stat cards: value, bar, and exactly how much headroom is left
                // before this stat breaks one of the two gyms.
                const limits = (statLimits(chosen, allStats)||{rows:[]}).rows;
                const COLOUR = { "whoring this":"#7abf7a", "middle":"#7a9abf", "leave behind":"#7a5a5a" };
                for (const L of limits) {
                    const v      = allStats[L.key];
                    const pct    = Math.max(1, Math.round(v / maxStat * 100));
                    const colour = COLOUR[L.role] || "#7a9abf";
                    // The headline sentence, phrased the way the stat's job demands
                    let verdict, vcol;
                    if (L.max != null && L.max <= 0) {
                        // Negative ceiling: no value of THIS stat can satisfy the pair
                        // gym, so it isn't the culprit — the whored stat has run away.
                        verdict = `Not this stat's fault — even at zero it can't open ${A.pairGym?.name || "the pair gym"}. ${STAT_ABBREV[A.P.whore]} has run too far ahead; bring the middle stats up.`;
                        vcol = "#bf7a5a";
                    } else if (L.min != null && v < L.min) {
                        verdict = `${STAT_LABELS[L.key]} is ${fmtK(L.min - v)} below the minimum — train it.`;
                        vcol = "#bf7a5a";
                    } else if (L.max != null && v > L.max) {
                        // A middle/forgotten stat over its ceiling usually means the
                        // whored stat is lagging, not that this stat is "wrong" —
                        // and you can't un-train it, so point at the real fix.
                        verdict = L.role === "whoring this"
                            ? `${STAT_LABELS[L.key]} is ${fmtK(v - L.max)} ABOVE the limit — it's what's shutting ${A.pairGym?.name || "the pair gym"}.`
                            : `${STAT_LABELS[L.key]} is ${fmtK(v - L.max)} above where it can sit while ${STAT_ABBREV[A.P.whore]} is only ${fmt(allStats[A.P.whore])}. Train ${STAT_ABBREV[A.P.whore]} — don't touch this.`;
                        vcol = "#bf5a5a";
                    } else if (L.max != null) {
                        verdict = `${STAT_LABELS[L.key]} is ${fmtK(L.max - v)} below the limit.`;
                        vcol = "#7abf7a";
                    } else {
                        verdict = `${STAT_LABELS[L.key]} is ${fmtK(v - (L.min || 0))} above the minimum.`;
                        vcol = "#7abf7a";
                    }
                    html += `<div style="padding:6px 8px;margin-top:4px;border-radius:4px;background:#161616;border:1px solid ${L.ok ? "#242e24" : "#3a2424"}">
  <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:34px 82px 1fr auto;gap:0 8px;align-items:center">
    <div style="font-size:11px;color:${colour};font-weight:bold">${STAT_ABBREV[L.key]}</div>
    <div style="font-size:12px;color:#dde;text-align:right">${fmt(v)}</div>
    <div style="height:9px;background:#101018;border-radius:2px;overflow:hidden"><div style="height:100%;width:${pct}%;background:${colour};opacity:.8"></div></div>
    <div style="font-size:9px;color:#667;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.04em">${L.role}</div>
  </div>
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:${vcol};margin-top:4px;line-height:1.5">${L.ok ? "✓" : "✗"} ${verdict}</div>
  <div style="font-size:10px;color:#556;margin-top:2px;line-height:1.5">
    ${L.min != null ? `Min <strong>${fmt(L.min)}</strong>` : ""}${L.min != null && L.max != null && L.max > 0 ? " · " : ""}${L.max != null && L.max > 0 ? `Max <strong>${fmt(L.max)}</strong>` : ""}
    ${L.max != null && L.max <= 0 ? `<span style="color:#bf7a5a">No headroom — must be 0 at this spread</span>` : ""}
    — ${L.why}
  </div>
</div>`;
                }

                html += `<div style="font-size:11px;color:${A.inBand?"#7abf9a":"#bf9f5a"};margin:8px 0 4px;line-height:1.6">
  ${W} is <strong>${fmtD(A.ratio,2)}×</strong> your ${M.join("/")} average.
  ${A.inBand ? `✓ Inside the ${RATIO_MIN}–${RATIO_MAX} window — right where you want to be.`
             : A.ratio < RATIO_MIN ? `Needs to reach ${RATIO_MIN}× to open ${A.soloGym?.name}.`
                                   : `Above ${RATIO_MAX}× — drifting toward losing ${A.pairGym?.name} at ${RATIO_BREAK}×.`}
</div>`;

                // The two gyms this path is for
                html += `<div style="font-size:10px;color:#445;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.06em;margin:10px 0 4px">Your two target gyms</div>`;
                const gymLine = (gym, ok, dots, what, need) => `<div style="display:flex;align-items:baseline;gap:8px;padding:5px 8px;margin-top:3px;border-radius:3px;background:${ok?"#141e18":"#1e1818"};border:1px solid ${ok?"#254030":"#402525"}">
  <span style="font-size:12px">${ok?"✅":"🔒"}</span>
  <span style="font-size:12px;color:${ok?"#7abf7a":"#bf7a7a"};font-weight:bold;flex:1">${gym?.name || "?"}</span>
  <span style="font-size:10px;color:#667">${gym?.energy ?? "?"}E · ${dots} dots ${what}</span>
</div>${need ? `<div style="font-size:11px;color:#bf9f5a;padding:2px 0 0 26px">${need}</div>` : ""}`;
                html += gymLine(A.soloGym, A.soloOk, A.soloGym?.[STAT_KEYS[A.P.whore]] ?? "?", W,
                    A.soloOk ? null : `Train ${STAT_ABBREV[A.P.whore]} to <strong>${fmt(floorV)}</strong> — ${fmt(Math.max(0, floorV - cur))} more.`);
                html += gymLine(A.pairGym, A.pairOk, 7.5, M.join("/"),
                    A.pairOk ? null : `${M.join("+")} must be 25% above ${W}+${F}. Train ${M.join(" and ")}, not ${W}.`);

                // The single most useful line: the band to hold
                html += `<div style="font-size:10px;color:#445;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.06em;margin:10px 0 4px">What to do next</div>`;
                if (!A.feasible) {
                    // Floor above ceiling — the forgotten stat is already too big.
                    const perMid = Math.ceil((A.midNeeded - A.midSum) / 2);
                    html += `<div style="padding:9px 11px;background:#1e1818;border:1px solid #402525;border-radius:4px;font-size:11px;color:#bf8a8a;line-height:1.7">
  <strong>This path can't open at your current spread.</strong><br>
  Your ${F} is already ${fmt(A.forgetSum)} combined, and you can't un-train a stat in Torn.
  ${A.soloGym?.name} wants ${W} above <strong>${fmt(floorV)}</strong>, while ${A.pairGym?.name} wants ${W}+${F} low enough that ${W} stays under <strong>${fmt(ceilV)}</strong> — there's no number that satisfies both.<br>
  <strong>Two ways out:</strong><br>
  · Grow ${M.join(" and ")} by about <strong>${fmt(Math.max(0,perMid))} each</strong> (to ${fmt(A.midNeeded)} combined) — that widens the band until it reopens.<br>
  · Or pick the path whose "forget" stat is your genuinely smallest — that's the cheaper fix if you haven't committed yet.
</div>`;
                } else if (A.soloOk && A.pairOk) {
                    html += `<div style="padding:9px 11px;background:#0a1a14;border:1px solid #1a3a28;border-radius:4px;font-size:11px;color:#7abf9a;line-height:1.7">
  <strong>Both gyms are open — hold this shape.</strong><br>
  Keep ${W} between <strong>${fmt(floorV)}</strong> and <strong>${fmt(ceilV)}</strong>.<br>
  Below ${fmt(floorV)} you lose ${A.soloGym?.name}. Above ${fmt(ceilV)} you lose ${A.pairGym?.name}.<br>
  Day to day: train <strong>${M.join(" and ")}</strong> together, and top up ${W} only when it nears the floor.
</div>`;
                } else if (A.ratio < RATIO_MIN) {
                    html += `<div style="padding:9px 11px;background:#1a1408;border:1px solid #3a2818;border-radius:4px;font-size:11px;color:#bf9f5a;line-height:1.7">
  <strong>Train ${STAT_LABELS[A.P.whore]} until it hits ${fmt(floorV)}</strong> (${fmt(Math.max(0, floorV - cur))} to go).<br>
  Don't train ${M.join(" or ")} while you do this — every point you add there raises the bar you're chasing.<br>
  Leave ${F} at zero.
</div>`;
                } else {
                    html += `<div style="padding:9px 11px;background:#1a1408;border:1px solid #3a2818;border-radius:4px;font-size:11px;color:#bf9f5a;line-height:1.7">
  <strong>${W} has run too far ahead.</strong> Train <strong>${M.join(" and ")}</strong> until ${W} is back under ${fmt(ceilV)},
  or ${A.pairGym?.name} stays shut. Stop training ${W} for now.
</div>`;
                }

                html += `<div style="font-size:10px;color:#667;margin-top:8px;line-height:1.6">
  ⚠ Committing is close to permanent — reversing means out-training the whole gap. ${A.P.forget.map(k=>STAT_LABELS[k]).join(" and ")} stay near zero until you're done;
  the book <em>Ignorance Is Bliss</em> (happy regen above max for 31 days) is how people backfill it later.
</div>`;
                if ((p.xanTaken + p.extTaken) <= 50) {
                    html += `<div style="font-size:10px;color:#7a5a3a;margin-top:5px;line-height:1.6">
  Sports Science Lab (9.0 dots, all stats) needs ≤50 lifetime Xanax + Ecstasy — you're at ${fmt(p.xanTaken + p.extTaken)}.
  Keeping it means no drug energy at all, which is a far bigger loss than the extra dots.
</div>`;
                }
            }

            // ── Reference: every ratio gym and its exact requirement ──────────
            html += `<div style="font-size:10px;color:#445;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.06em;margin:12px 0 4px">All specialist gyms — what each needs</div>`;
            for (const id of [25, 26, 27, 28, 29, 30, 31]) {
                const gym = GYM_BY_ID[id], req = SPECIALIST_REQS[id];
                if (!gym || !req) continue;
                const ok  = id === 31 ? (p.xanTaken + p.extTaken) <= 50 : req.check(allStats);
                const dots = Math.max(gym.str||0, gym.spd||0, gym.def||0, gym.dex||0);
                html += `<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:16px 1fr auto;gap:0 6px;padding:3px 0;border-bottom:1px solid #1a1a22;align-items:baseline">
  <div style="font-size:10px">${ok ? "✅" : "🔒"}</div>
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:${ok ? "#8abf8a" : "#778"}">${gym.name} <span style="font-size:10px;color:#556">${gym.energy}E · ${dots} dots</span><br>
    <span style="font-size:10px;color:#667">${req.desc}</span></div>
</div>`;
            }
        }

        // ── eDVD trade-off — buy more, or fill with candy? ────────────────────
        const tr = edvdTradeoff(c, p);
        if (tr.rows.length > 1) {
            const ownedTxt = tr.owned == null ? "unknown" : `${tr.owned}`;
            const best  = tr.rows.reduce((a,b)=> b.weeklyGain > a.weeklyGain ? b : a, tr.rows[0]);
            const stock = tr.rows.filter(r => r.buy === 0).reduce((a,b)=> !a || b.weeklyGain > a.weeklyGain ? b : a, null);
            html += rsec(`eDVD trade-off — you own ${ownedTxt}, cap allows ${tr.maxEdvd}`);
            html += `<div style="font-size:11px;color:#667;margin-bottom:6px;line-height:1.5">
  Every row is a full 4-Xanax jump with candy filling whatever booster room the eDVDs leave.
  "Buy" is what you'd still have to purchase.
</div>`;
            html += `<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:44px 46px 62px 60px 58px;gap:0 6px;margin-bottom:2px">
  ${["eDVD","Candy","Happy","Gain/wk","To buy"].map((h,i)=>`<div style="font-size:9px;color:#445;text-transform:uppercase;text-align:${i?'right':'left'};border-bottom:1px solid #1e1e2a;padding-bottom:3px">${h}</div>`).join("")}
</div>`;
            for (const r of tr.rows) {
                const isBest  = r === best, isStock = stock && r === stock;
                const col = isBest ? "#7abf7a" : isStock ? "#8abfcf" : "#99a";
                const tag = isBest ? ` <span style="font-size:9px;color:#3a7a3a">best</span>`
                          : isStock ? ` <span style="font-size:9px;color:#3a6a7a">from stock</span>` : "";
                html += `<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:44px 46px 62px 60px 58px;gap:0 6px;padding:4px 0;border-bottom:1px solid #1a1a22;align-items:baseline">
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:${col};font-weight:${isBest?'bold':'normal'}">${r.e}${tag}</div>
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:#778;text-align:right">${r.candies||"—"}</div>
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:#778;text-align:right">${fmt(r.sim.happy)}</div>
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:${col};text-align:right">+${fmt(r.weeklyGain)}</div>
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:${r.buy?'#bf9f5a':'#445'};text-align:right">${r.buy?`${r.buy}× ${fmtBig(r.cost.gap)}`:"—"}</div>
</div>`;
            }
            // The actual verdict on buying up
            if (stock && best && best !== stock) {
                const dGain = best.weeklyGain - stock.weeklyGain;
                const dCost = best.cost.gap - stock.cost.gap;
                const pct   = stock.weeklyGain > 0 ? (dGain / stock.weeklyGain * 100) : 0;
                const perM  = dCost > 0 ? dGain / (dCost / 1e6) : Infinity;
                const worth = perM >= (stock.weeklyCost > 0 ? stock.weeklyGain / (stock.weeklyCost/1e6) : 0);
                html += `<div style="margin-top:8px;padding:9px 11px;border-radius:4px;background:${worth?'#0a1a14':'#1a1408'};border:1px solid ${worth?'#1a3a28':'#3a2818'};font-size:11px;color:${worth?'#7abf9a':'#bf9f5a'};line-height:1.7">
  <strong>Jump now with ${stock.e} eDVD + ${stock.candies} candy</strong> → +${fmt(stock.weeklyGain)}/week, nothing to buy.<br>
  <strong>Buy up to ${best.e} eDVD</strong> (${best.buy}× for ${fmtBig(dCost)}) → +${fmt(best.weeklyGain)}/week, <strong>${pct>0?"+":""}${pct.toFixed(0)}% more</strong>.<br>
  That's ${isFinite(perM)?`${fmt(perM)} ${c.statLabel} per extra $1m`:"free"} — ${worth
      ? `better than the rest of the jump earns, so buying up is worth it.`
      : `worse than the jump already earns per dollar. <strong>Don't buy the extra eDVDs</strong> — candy the gap and jump now.`}
</div>`;
            } else if (stock && best === stock) {
                html += `<div style="margin-top:8px;padding:9px 11px;border-radius:4px;background:#0a1a14;border:1px solid #1a3a28;font-size:11px;color:#7abf9a;line-height:1.7">
  ✓ Your ${stock.e} eDVD + ${stock.candies} candy is already the best row here — buying more eDVDs would cost cycle time for less gain. Jump as you are.
</div>`;
            }
        }

        // ── Ready-now callout ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
        if (rec.readyNow) {
            const fromStock = rec.readyNow.cost.gap === 0;
            html += `<div style="margin-top:8px;padding:9px 11px;background:#0a1a14;border:1px solid #1a3a28;border-radius:4px;font-size:11px;color:#7abf9a;line-height:1.6">
  ✅ <strong>You can run this right now:</strong> <strong>${rec.readyNow.t.name}</strong>${fromStock
      ? ` straight out of your inventory — nothing to buy, no cooldown in the way`
      : ` once you buy ${fmtBig(rec.readyNow.cost.gap)} of items (you have ${fmtBig(rec.cashNow)})`} — +${fmt(rec.readyNow.sim.gain)} ${c.statLabel} this session.
  ${rec.readyNow !== R ? `<br><span style="color:#5a8a7a">Note: that's above your sustainable weekly budget — fine as a one-off, not as a routine.</span>` : ""}
</div>`;
        } else if (R.blockers.length) {
            html += `<div style="margin-top:8px;padding:9px 11px;background:#1a1408;border:1px solid #3a2818;border-radius:4px;font-size:11px;color:#bf9f5a;line-height:1.6">
  ⏳ <strong>Not ready yet:</strong> ${R.blockers.join(" · ")}
</div>`;
        }

        // ── Action plan ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        html += rsec("Your plan");
        const steps = [];

        // Free wins first — gym, perks, happy
        const statKey = STAT_KEYS[c.stat];
        const bestOwnedDots = Math.max(...GYMS.filter(g => {
            if (SPECIALIST_IDS.has(g.id)) return JSON.parse(_load('manualGymOwned','[]')).includes(g.id);
            return (STANDARD_UNLOCK_ORDER[g.id] ?? 99) <= (STANDARD_UNLOCK_ORDER[c.gym.id] ?? 0);
        }).map(g => g[statKey] || 0), 0);
        if (bestOwnedDots > c.dots + 0.01) {
            const bg = GYMS.find(g => Math.abs((g[statKey]||0) - bestOwnedDots) < 0.01);
            steps.push(`<strong style="color:#bf9f5a">Switch gyms first.</strong> ${bg?.name} gives ${bestOwnedDots} dots vs ${c.dots} here — that's ${(((bestOwnedDots/c.dots)-1)*100).toFixed(0)}% more per energy, free.`);
        }
        const missingPerks = [];
        if (getPerk(el.factionPerk)  === 0) missingPerks.push("faction gym perk");
        if (getPerk(el.propertyPerk) === 0) missingPerks.push("property gym perk");
        if (getPerk(el.eduStatPerk) === 0 && getPerk(el.eduGenPerk) === 0) missingPerks.push("education gym courses");
        if (getPerk(el.jobPerk)      === 0) missingPerks.push("company job perk");
        if (missingPerks.length) steps.push(`<strong style="color:#bf9f5a">Free multipliers you're missing:</strong> ${missingPerks.join(", ")}. These stack multiplicatively with everything below — worth more long-term than any single jump.`);
        if (c.propHappy < 4000) steps.push(`<strong style="color:#bf9f5a">Upgrade your property.</strong> Max happy ${fmt(c.propHappy)} is low — happy drives the whole formula, and a bigger base multiplies every jump.`);

        steps.push(`<strong style="color:#7abf9a">Run ${R.t.name}.</strong> ${
            R.t.kind==="jump"  ? `<strong>Timing matters more than anything else here.</strong> Happy above your property max wipes every 15 minutes (XX:00, XX:15, XX:30, XX:45), so the whole stack has to go in just AFTER a tick, then straight to the gym. Order: candy → eDVD → ecstasy LAST (it doubles whatever you've built) → train it all off in ${c.gym.name} in one click, since happy drops as you train. Stack your Xanax energy in the hours beforehand — that part isn't time-sensitive.`
          : R.t.kind==="drug"  ? `Train at full energy, take your ${R.t.drug==="lsd"?"LSD":"Xanax"}, train again — two trains per cooldown cycle, ${R.t.doses}× a day.`
          :                      `Train every time you hit ${c.cap}E so you never waste regen.`}`);
        if (R.weeklyCost > 0 && income.weekly != null && income.weekly > 0)
            steps.push(`Budget <strong>${fmtBig(R.weeklyCost)}/week</strong> for this — ${((R.weeklyCost/income.weekly)*100).toFixed(0)}% of your income. Keep ${fmtBig(rec.reserve)} in reserve.`);
        if (rec.nextStep)
            steps.push(`When your income reaches <strong>${fmtBig(rec.nextStep.weeklyCost/rec.spendPct)}/week</strong>, move up to <strong>${rec.nextStep.t.name}</strong>.`);
        if (c.statGoal > c.statNow) {
            const wks = R.weeklyGain > 0 ? (c.statGoal - c.statNow) / R.weeklyGain : Infinity;
            steps.push(`At this rate you hit your ${fmt(c.statGoal)} goal in <strong>${isFinite(wks)?`${wks.toFixed(1)} weeks`:"never"}</strong>${isFinite(wks)?` (${dateIn(wks*7)})`:""}.`);
        }
        // Jumps stop paying off as stats climb — published break-even is ~400k per
        // stat, extending to ~800k if you work a 10-star Adult Novelties shop
        // (which doubles eDVD happy). Worth saying before someone buys a stack.
        const jumpBreakeven = c.anJob ? 800000 : 400000;
        if (R.t.kind === "jump" && c.statNow > jumpBreakeven) {
            steps.push(`<strong style="color:#bf9f5a">Heads up:</strong> at ${fmt(c.statNow)} ${c.statLabel} you're past the ~${fmt(jumpBreakeven)} point where jumps stop roughly doubling your daily gains.${c.anJob?"":` A 10★ Adult Novelties job doubles eDVD happy and pushes that break-even to ~800k.`} Grinding may now be the better spend.`);
        } else if (R.t.kind !== "jump" && c.statNow < jumpBreakeven) {
            steps.push(`You're under ~${fmt(jumpBreakeven)} ${c.statLabel}, which is where jumps pay best — they roughly double daily gains at this level. Worth saving toward one.`);
        }
        steps.push(`Use <strong>⚖ Compare</strong> for the exact jump-then-switch schedule once you're running this.`);

        html += `<ol style="margin:4px 0 0;padding-left:20px;font-size:11px;color:#99a;line-height:1.8">${steps.map(s=>`<li style="margin-bottom:5px">${s}</li>`).join("")}</ol>`;

        html += `<div style="font-size:9px;color:#334;text-align:right;margin-top:10px;padding-top:5px;border-top:1px solid #1a1a22">Assessed ${new Date().toLocaleTimeString()} · prices from weav3r.dev · income from ${income.source}</div>`;

        // Wrap in a collapsible so a long assessment can be folded away once read,
        // with the headline (pick + gain + cost) still visible in the header.
        const hdr = `${R.t.name} · +${fmt(R.weeklyGain)}/wk · ${R.weeklyCost === 0 ? "free" : fmtBig(R.weeklyCost) + "/wk"}`;
        // Native <details> so it collapses without wiring, with the headline
        // kept in the summary so a folded assessment is still informative.
        out.innerHTML = `<details class="gg-dis" id="gg-assess-result" open>
  <summary style="color:var(--accent)">Assessment &mdash; ${hdr}</summary>
  <div class="inner">${html}</div>
</details>`;
    }

    q("#gg-assess-run")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
        runAssessment().catch(e => {
            if (el.assessOut) el.assessOut.innerHTML = `<div class="gg-status warn" style="display:block">✗ Assessment failed: ${e.message}</div>`;
        });
    });

    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // BUILD PAGE — Plan → Build path
    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // Renders the chosen specialisation against real stats. The wording matters
    // more than the maths here: the whored stat has a FLOOR to train toward,
    // everything else has a CEILING that is slack. Calling both "the limit"
    // (as the reference script does) is the single most confusing thing about
    // these builds, so each card says which kind it has and what to do.
    function renderBuildPage() {
        const out = q("#gg-build-out"); if (!out) return;
        const hasStats = allStats.strength > 0 || allStats.speed > 0 ||
                         allStats.defense  > 0 || allStats.dexterity > 0;
        if (!hasStats) {
            out.innerHTML = `<div class="gg-note info" style="line-height:1.7">
  <strong>Need your stats first.</strong> Press <strong>&#8635;</strong> in the header, or type them in under &#9881; Setup &#8594; Profile.
</div>`;
            return;
        }

        const chosen = el.buildPath?.value || suggestPath(allStats)?.key;
        const L = chosen ? statLimits(chosen, allStats) : null;
        const A = chosen ? analysePath(chosen, allStats) : null;
        if (!L || !A) { out.innerHTML = ""; return; }

        const P = BUILD_PATHS[chosen];
        const maxStat = Math.max(...Object.keys(STAT_KEYS).map(k => allStats[k]), 1);
        const ROLE_COL = { "whoring this":"var(--accent)", "combo partner":"var(--info)",
                           "middle":"var(--info)", "leave behind":"var(--faint)", "keep low":"var(--faint)" };

        let html = "";
        if (!el.buildPath?.value) {
            html += `<div class="gg-note info" style="line-height:1.65">
  No path chosen, so this is the one your stats already fit best. Pick it on the <strong>Now</strong> tab to lock it in.
</div>`;
        }
        html += `<div class="gg-note ok" style="line-height:1.65">
  <strong>${P.label}</strong><br>
  ${P.ratio === "Baldr" ? "Baldr's Ratio &mdash; the solo gym shares a stat with the combo gym, so you double down on one stat instead of spreading across three."
   : P.ratio === "Hank" ? "Hank's Ratio &mdash; the two gyms cover three different stats between them."
   : "Combo gym only &mdash; no solo gym on this path."}
</div>`;

        html += `<div class="gg-lab">Your stats against their limits</div>`;
        for (const r of L.rows) {
            const v   = allStats[r.key];
            const pct = Math.max(1, Math.round(v / maxStat * 100));
            const col = ROLE_COL[r.role] || "var(--info)";
            const vcol = r.ok ? (r.near ? "var(--warn)" : "var(--accent)") : "var(--bad)";
            const line = r.bound === "floor"
                ? (r.tooLow ? `<strong>Train ${fmtK(-r.headroom)} more</strong> to reach ${fmt(r.min)}.`
                            : `Safe &mdash; <strong>${fmtK(r.headroom)} clear</strong> of the ${fmt(r.min)} minimum.`)
                : (r.tooHigh ? `<strong>${fmtK(-r.headroom)} over</strong> its ceiling.`
                             : `<strong>Room to grow: ${fmtK(r.headroom)}</strong>${r.max > 0 ? ` <span style="color:var(--muted)">&mdash; cap ${fmt(r.max)}</span>` : ""}`);
            html += `<div class="gg-stat">
  <div class="top">
    <span class="abbr" style="color:${col}">${STAT_ABBREV[r.key]}</span>
    <span class="num">${fmt(v)}</span>
    <span class="role">${r.role}</span>
  </div>
  <div class="bar"><i style="width:${pct}%;background:${col}"></i></div>
  <div class="verdict" style="color:${vcol}">${r.ok ? "" : "&#10007; "}${line}</div>
  <div class="why">${r.bound === "floor" ? "Has a <em>floor</em> &mdash; something to train toward. " : "Has a <em>ceiling</em> &mdash; slack, not a target. "}${r.why}</div>
</div>`;
        }

        html += `<div class="gg-note info" style="line-height:1.65">
  <strong>&ldquo;Room to grow&rdquo; is slack, not a target.</strong> You never train toward it &mdash; it's how far a stat can drift before it shuts a gym. Only the whored stat has something to train <em>toward</em>.
</div>`;

        if (P.whore) {
            html += `<div class="gg-lab">How to get there</div>`;
            const soloU  = unlockSteps(SOLO_GYM_FOR[P.whore], allStats);
            const comboU = unlockSteps(COMBO_GYMS[P.combo].id, allStats);
            for (const u of [soloU, comboU]) {
                if (!u) continue;
                const left = u.steps.filter(x => !x.done).length;
                html += `<details class="gg-dis" ${left ? "open" : ""} style="margin-bottom:6px">
  <summary style="color:${u.allDone ? "var(--accent)" : "var(--text)"}">
    ${u.gym.name} &mdash; ${u.allDone ? "all requirements met" : left + " step" + (left === 1 ? "" : "s") + " left"}
  </summary>
  <div class="inner">${renderStepList(u)}</div>
</details>`;
            }
            html += `<div class="gg-lab">Your two target gyms</div>`;
            const gymLine = (gym, ok, what) => `<div class="gg-gym${ok ? " open" : ""}">
  <span class="dot"></span><span class="nm">${gym?.name || "?"}</span>
  <span class="meta">${gym?.energy ?? "?"}E &#183; ${what}</span>
</div>`;
            // Only call a gym open if the path can actually hold both at once —
            // otherwise the page contradicts its own verdict below.
            html += gymLine(L.soloGym,  L.soloOk  && A.feasible, `${L.soloGym?.[STAT_KEYS[P.whore]] ?? "?"} ${STAT_ABBREV[P.whore]}`);
