            html += gymLine(L.comboGym, L.comboOk && A.feasible, `7.5 ${STAT_ABBREV[COMBO_GYMS[P.combo].stat1]}/${STAT_ABBREV[COMBO_GYMS[P.combo].stat2]}`);

            html += `<div class="gg-lab">What to do next</div>`;
            if (!A.feasible) {
                const perMid = Math.ceil((A.midNeeded - A.midSum) / Math.max(A.P.mid.length, 1));
                html += `<div class="gg-note bad" style="line-height:1.7">
  <strong>This path can't open at your current spread.</strong>
  Your ${A.P.forget.map(k => STAT_ABBREV[k]).join("+")} is already ${fmt(A.forgetSum)} combined, and you can't un-train a stat in Torn.
  There's no value of ${STAT_ABBREV[P.whore]} that satisfies both gyms.<br>
  <strong>Two ways out:</strong> raise ${A.P.mid.map(k => STAT_ABBREV[k]).join(" + ")} to ${fmt(A.midNeeded)} combined
  (about ${fmt(Math.max(0, perMid))} more on each), or switch to the path whose abandoned stat is genuinely your smallest &mdash;
  right now that is <strong>${STAT_ABBREV[Object.keys(STAT_KEYS).reduce((lo,k)=>allStats[k]<allStats[lo]?k:lo)]}</strong>.
</div>`;
            } else if (L.soloOk && L.comboOk) {
                html += `<div class="gg-note ok" style="line-height:1.7">
  <strong>Both gyms are open &mdash; hold this shape.</strong><br>
  ${P.ratio === "Baldr"
    ? `Keep training ${STAT_ABBREV[P.whore]}; it has no ceiling on this path. Let ${A.P.forget.map(k=>STAT_ABBREV[k]).join(" and ")} sit.`
    : `Keep ${STAT_ABBREV[P.whore]} between ${fmt(A.needWhore)} and ${fmt(A.maxWhore)}. Train ${A.P.mid.map(k=>STAT_ABBREV[k]).join(" and ")} together and top up ${STAT_ABBREV[P.whore]} only when it nears the floor.`}
</div>`;
            } else if (!L.soloOk) {
                html += `<div class="gg-note warn" style="line-height:1.7">
  <strong>Train ${STAT_LABELS[P.whore]} to ${fmt(A.needWhore)}</strong> &mdash; ${fmt(Math.max(0, A.needWhore - allStats[P.whore]))} to go &mdash; to open ${L.soloGym?.name}.<br>
  Don't train ${A.P.mid.map(k=>STAT_ABBREV[k]).join(" or ")} meanwhile: every point there raises the bar you're chasing.
</div>`;
            } else {
                html += `<div class="gg-note warn" style="line-height:1.7">
  <strong>${STAT_ABBREV[P.whore]} has run too far ahead.</strong> Train ${A.P.mid.map(k=>STAT_ABBREV[k]).join(" and ")} until it's back under ${fmt(A.maxWhore)}, or ${L.comboGym?.name} stays shut.
</div>`;
            }
        }

        html += `<div class="gg-lab">All specialist gyms</div>`;
        for (const id of [25, 26, 27, 28, 29, 30, 31]) {
            const gym = GYM_BY_ID[id], req = SPECIALIST_REQS[id];
            if (!gym || !req) continue;
            const ok = id === 31
                ? (player ? (player.xanTaken + player.extTaken) <= 50 : false)
                : req.check(allStats);
            const dots = Math.max(gym.str||0, gym.spd||0, gym.def||0, gym.dex||0);
            html += `<div class="gg-gym${ok ? " open" : ""}">
  <span class="dot"></span>
  <span class="nm">${gym.name}<br><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--faint)">${req.desc}</span></span>
  <span class="meta">${gym.energy}E &#183; ${dots}</span>
</div>`;
        }

        html += `<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--faint);line-height:1.6;margin-top:4px">
  Committing is close to permanent &mdash; reversing means out-training the whole gap.
  The book <em>Ignorance Is Bliss</em> is how people backfill an abandoned stat later.
</div>`;

        out.innerHTML = html;
    }

    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // UNLOCK CHAIN — what actually stands between you and a specialist gym
    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // Ratios are only half the story. Every specialist gym also needs a PREREQ
    // gym unlocked, and the standard gyms unlock sequentially by training in
    // them — so "get DEX to 628,291" is useless advice if you haven't reached
    // George's yet. This turns both halves into one ordered checklist.
    function unlockSteps(gymId, stats) {
        const req  = SPECIALIST_REQS[gymId];
        const gym  = GYM_BY_ID[gymId];
        if (!req || !gym) return null;
        const curId  = parseInt(el.gym?.value) || 0;
        const curPos = STANDARD_UNLOCK_ORDER[curId] ?? 0;
        const preId  = req.prereqId;
        const preGym = GYM_BY_ID[preId];
        const prePos = STANDARD_UNLOCK_ORDER[preId] ?? 99;
        const owned  = JSON.parse(_load("manualGymOwned", "[]"));
        const steps  = [];

        // 1. Walk the standard chain up to the prerequisite.
        if (curPos < prePos) {
            const togo = GYMS.filter(g => {
                const p = STANDARD_UNLOCK_ORDER[g.id];
                return p !== undefined && p > curPos && p <= prePos;
            }).sort((a, b) => STANDARD_UNLOCK_ORDER[a.id] - STANDARD_UNLOCK_ORDER[b.id]);
            steps.push({
                done: false, key: "chain",
                title: `Unlock ${preGym?.name || "the prerequisite gym"}`,
                detail: `${togo.length} gym${togo.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} to go from ${GYM_BY_ID[curId]?.name || "here"} — ` +
                        `they unlock in order as you train: ${togo.map(g => g.name).join(" → ")}.`,
            });
        } else {
            steps.push({ done: true, key: "chain",
                title: `${preGym?.name || "Prerequisite"} unlocked`,
                detail: `Required before ${gym.name} can be bought.` });
        }

        // 2. The ratio itself, expressed as a number to hit.
        const ratioOk = req.check(stats);
        if (SPECIALIST_IDS.has(gymId) && gymId !== 31) {
            const isPair = gymId === 25 || gymId === 26;
            let detail;
            if (isPair) {
                const C = gymId === 25 ? COMBO_GYMS.balboas : COMBO_GYMS.frontline;
                const need = (stats[C.sec1] + stats[C.sec2]) * RATIO_MIN;
                const have = stats[C.stat1] + stats[C.stat2];
                detail = ratioOk
                    ? `${STAT_ABBREV[C.stat1]}+${STAT_ABBREV[C.stat2]} is ${fmt(have)} against the ${fmt(need)} needed.`
                    : `${STAT_ABBREV[C.stat1]}+${STAT_ABBREV[C.stat2]} must reach ${fmt(need)} — ${fmt(need - have)} more between them.`;
            } else {
                const stat = { 27:"strength", 28:"defense", 29:"speed", 30:"dexterity" }[gymId];
                const others = Object.keys(STAT_KEYS).filter(k => k !== stat).map(k => stats[k]);
                const need = Math.max(...others) * RATIO_MIN;
                detail = ratioOk
                    ? `${STAT_ABBREV[stat]} is ${fmt(stats[stat])}, clear of the ${fmt(need)} minimum.`
                    : `Train ${STAT_ABBREV[stat]} to ${fmt(need)} — ${fmt(need - stats[stat])} more.`;
            }
            steps.push({ done: ratioOk, key: "ratio", title: req.desc.split("·")[0].trim(), detail });
        } else if (gymId === 31) {
            const taken = player ? player.xanTaken + player.extTaken : null;
            steps.push({ done: taken != null && taken <= 50, key: "ratio",
                title: "Stay under 50 lifetime Xanax + Ecstasy",
                detail: taken == null ? "Run an assessment to read your lifetime drug counts."
                      : taken <= 50 ? `${fmt(taken)} of 50 used.`
                      : `${fmt(taken)} used — permanently past the limit.` });
        }

        // 3. Buying it.
        const bought = owned.includes(gymId);
        steps.push({ done: bought, key: "buy",
            title: `Buy ${gym.name}`,
            detail: bought ? "Marked as owned." : "Specialist gyms are a separate purchase once the ratio is met." });
        return { gym, steps, allDone: steps.every(s => s.done) };
    }

    function renderStepList(u) {
        if (!u) return "";
        return u.steps.map((s, i) => `<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:18px 1fr;gap:8px;padding:6px 0;border-bottom:1px solid #1c2028">
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:${s.done ? "var(--accent)" : "var(--faint)"};font-family:var(--mono)">${s.done ? "&#10003;" : i + 1}</div>
  <div>
    <div style="font-size:11px;color:${s.done ? "var(--muted)" : "var(--text)"}">${s.title}</div>
    <div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--faint);line-height:1.5;margin-top:2px">${s.detail}</div>
  </div>
</div>`).join("");
    }

    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // GYM TRACKER — every gym, what unlocks it, and which build wants it
    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    function renderGymTracker() {
        const out = q("#gg-gyms-out"); if (!out) return;
        const curId  = parseInt(el.gym?.value) || 0;
        const curPos = STANDARD_UNLOCK_ORDER[curId] ?? 0;
        const owned  = JSON.parse(_load("manualGymOwned", "[]"));
        const stat   = el.stat?.value || "strength";
        const sk     = STAT_KEYS[stat];
        const hasStats = allStats.strength > 0 || allStats.dexterity > 0;

        // Which build paths would want each specialist gym
        const wants = {};
        for (const [k, P] of Object.entries(BUILD_PATHS)) {
            const ids = [P.whore ? SOLO_GYM_FOR[P.whore] : null, COMBO_GYMS[P.combo].id].filter(Boolean);
            ids.forEach(id => { (wants[id] = wants[id] || []).push(P.label.replace(/ \(.*\)/, "")); });
        }

        let html = `<div class="gg-note info" style="line-height:1.6">
  Standard gyms unlock in order just by training in them. The seven specialist gyms each need a
  prerequisite gym <em>and</em> a stat ratio, then a separate purchase.
</div>`;

        const rowFor = (g, state, note) => {
            const dots = g[sk] || 0;
            const col  = state === "owned" ? "var(--accent)" : state === "next" ? "var(--warn)" : "var(--faint)";
            const mark = state === "owned" ? "&#10003;" : state === "next" ? "&#9656;" : "&#9679;";
            return `<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:16px 1fr auto;gap:8px;padding:6px 0;border-bottom:1px solid #1c2028;align-items:baseline">
  <div style="color:${col};font-size:10px">${mark}</div>
  <div>
    <div style="font-size:12px;color:${state === "locked" ? "var(--muted)" : "var(--text)"}">${g.name}
      ${wants[g.id] ? `<span style="font-size:9px;color:var(--info)"> ${wants[g.id].length} build${wants[g.id].length>1?"s":""}</span>` : ""}</div>
    ${note ? `<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--faint);line-height:1.5;margin-top:2px">${note}</div>` : ""}
  </div>
  <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);text-align:right">${g.energy}E<br>${dots || "—"} ${STAT_ABBREV[stat]}</div>
</div>`;
        };

        html += `<div class="gg-lab">Standard gyms — unlock in order</div>`;
        const std = GYMS.filter(g => STANDARD_UNLOCK_ORDER[g.id] !== undefined && g.id !== 32)
                        .sort((a, b) => STANDARD_UNLOCK_ORDER[a.id] - STANDARD_UNLOCK_ORDER[b.id]);
        for (const g of std) {
            const p = STANDARD_UNLOCK_ORDER[g.id];
            const state = p <= curPos ? "owned" : p === curPos + 1 ? "next" : "locked";
            html += rowFor(g, state,
                state === "owned" ? (g.id === curId ? "You're training here" : "")
              : state === "next"  ? "Next to unlock — keep training"
              : `${p - curPos} gyms away`);
        }

        html += `<div class="gg-lab" style="margin-top:8px">Specialist gyms — ratio + purchase</div>`;
        for (const id of [25, 26, 27, 28, 29, 30, 31]) {
            const g = GYM_BY_ID[id]; if (!g) continue;
            const u = hasStats ? unlockSteps(id, allStats) : null;
            const state = owned.includes(id) ? "owned" : (u && u.steps.filter(s => !s.done).length === 1) ? "next" : "locked";
            const blocker = !hasStats ? "Press &#8635; to check against your stats"
                          : u ? (u.allDone ? "All requirements met — buy it"
                                           : u.steps.filter(s => !s.done).map(s => s.title).join(" · "))
                              : "";
            html += rowFor(g, state, blocker);
            if (hasStats && u && !owned.includes(id)) {
                html += `<details class="gg-dis" style="margin:4px 0 8px">
  <summary>What ${g.name} needs</summary>
  <div class="inner">${renderStepList(u)}
    <button class="gg-btn sm gg-own-btn" data-gymid="${id}">I own this gym</button>
  </div>
</details>`;
            }
        }

        out.innerHTML = html;
        out.querySelectorAll(".gg-own-btn").forEach(b => b.addEventListener("click", () => {
            const id = parseInt(b.dataset.gymid);
            const list = JSON.parse(_load("manualGymOwned", "[]"));
            if (!list.includes(id)) list.push(id);
            _save("manualGymOwned", JSON.stringify(list));
            renderGymTracker(); renderBuildPage(); updateBuildBar();
        }));
    }

    // Build bar on the Now tab — mirrors the build page in one line.
    function updateBuildBar() {
        const box = q("#gg-build-state"); if (!box) return;
        const key = el.buildPath?.value;
        const hasStats = allStats.strength > 0 || allStats.dexterity > 0 ||
                         allStats.speed > 0 || allStats.defense > 0;
        const dot = c => `<span class="dot" style="background:${c}"></span>`;
        if (!key) {
            box.innerHTML = `${dot("var(--faint)")}<span>No path chosen &mdash; specialist gyms stay locked and you train on 7.3 dots.</span>`;
        } else if (!hasStats) {
            box.innerHTML = `${dot("var(--faint)")}<span>Press &#8635; to check this path against your stats.</span>`;
        } else {
            const L = statLimits(key, allStats), A = analysePath(key, allStats);
            const P = BUILD_PATHS[key];
            if (!L || !A) return;
            if (!A.feasible) {
                box.innerHTML = `${dot("var(--bad)")}<span>Can't open at your current spread &mdash; ${A.P.forget.map(k=>STAT_ABBREV[k]).join("+")} is already too high.</span>`;
            } else if (L.soloOk && L.comboOk) {
                box.innerHTML = `${dot("var(--accent)")}<span>${L.soloGym?.name} and ${L.comboGym?.name} both open &mdash; train <strong>${STAT_ABBREV[P.whore]}</strong>.</span>`;
            } else if (!L.soloOk) {
                box.innerHTML = `${dot("var(--warn)")}<span>${L.comboGym?.name} open. ${L.soloGym?.name} needs <strong>${STAT_ABBREV[P.whore]} ${fmt(A.needWhore)}</strong> &mdash; ${fmt(Math.max(0, A.needWhore - allStats[P.whore]))} to go.</span>`;
            } else {
                box.innerHTML = `${dot("var(--warn)")}<span>${L.soloGym?.name} open. ${STAT_ABBREV[P.whore]} is too far ahead for ${L.comboGym?.name}.</span>`;
            }
        }
        checkStatClash();
    }

    // The build path and the stat picker are separate controls that can disagree.
    // Training the wrong stat actively raises the floor the whored stat must clear.
    function checkStatClash() {
        const box = q("#gg-stat-clash"); if (!box) return;
        const key = el.buildPath?.value;
        const want = key ? BUILD_PATHS[key]?.whore : null;
        const have = el.stat?.value;
        if (!want || want === have) { box.style.display = "none"; return; }
        box.style.display = "block";
        box.innerHTML = `You're training <strong>${STAT_ABBREV[have]}</strong>, but this build whores <strong>${STAT_ABBREV[want]}</strong>. Every point of ${STAT_LABELS[have]} raises the floor ${STAT_ABBREV[want]} has to clear.
  <button class="gg-link" id="gg-fix-stat" style="color:var(--warn)">Switch to ${STAT_LABELS[want]}</button>`;
        q("#gg-fix-stat")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
            el.stat.value = want; gg_save("stat", want);
            updateDotsDisplay(); updateBestGymPanel(); checkStatClash(); maybeRecalc();
        });
    }

    el.buildPath?.addEventListener("change", () => { updateBuildBar(); renderBuildPage(); });
    el.stat?.addEventListener("change", checkStatClash);

    // Debug buttons
    const debugLog = [];
    const _ow = console.warn.bind(console);
    console.warn = (...a) => { _ow(...a); if (String(a[0]).includes("[AIO")) debugLog.push("W:"+a.join(" ")); };
    q("#gg-copy-logs")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
        const log = [
            "Gym Planner v3.0 " + new Date().toISOString(),
            "GYMS:" + GYMS.length + " maxSPD:" + Math.max(...GYMS.map(g=>g.spd||0)),
            "Jail:" + JSON.stringify(GYMS.find(g=>g.id===32)),
            "CanPrices:" + JSON.stringify(canPrices),
            "CAN_IDs:" + CAN_TYPES.map(c=>c.label+":"+c.id).join(","),
            "Stats:" + JSON.stringify(allStats), "ActivePanel:" + (typeof activePanel !== 'undefined' ? activePanel : 'n/a'),
            "---", ...debugLog.slice(-20),
        ].join("\n");
        GM_setClipboard ? GM_setClipboard(log) : navigator.clipboard?.writeText(log);
        const ds = q("#gg-debug-status");
        if (ds) { ds.style.display = "block"; ds.textContent = "✓ Copied"; setTimeout(() => ds.style.display = "none", 2000); }
    });
    q("#gg-clear-cache")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
        ["gymDataCache_v2","gymDataCacheTs_v2","gymDataCache","gymDataCacheTs",
         PRICE_CACHE_KEY,"canItemIds","canItemIdsTs","lastAutofillTs","manualGymOwned"]
         .forEach(k => { try { GM_deleteValue(k); } catch(_){} });
        CAN_TYPES.forEach(c => c.id = null); CANDY_TYPES.forEach(c => c.id = null);
        GYMS = GYMS_FALLBACK.slice(); buildGymMaps(); rebuildGymDropdown();
        const ds = q("#gg-debug-status");
        if (ds) { ds.style.display = "block"; ds.style.color = "#bf7a7a"; ds.textContent = "✓ Cleared"; setTimeout(() => ds.style.display = "none", 3000); }
        showStatus("ok", "✓ Cache cleared. Reload or Auto-fill.");
    });


})();
